<div class="flex items-center gap-2">


  <a class="tag-type-1 heading text-xl" href="/posts?tags=khyle.">khyle.</a>

  <span class="text-sm post-count">933</span>
</div>

  <div class="flex flex-col gap-2">
      <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
          <a class="artist-other-name chip-primary text-sm truncate" rel="nofollow" href="/artists?search%5Bany_name_matches%5D=Khyle.">Khyle.</a>
          <a class="artist-other-name chip-primary text-sm truncate" rel="nofollow" href="/artists?search%5Bany_name_matches%5D=irelyhk">irelyhk</a>
          <a class="artist-other-name chip-primary text-sm truncate" rel="nofollow" href="/artists?search%5Bany_name_matches%5D=khyle">khyle</a>

      </div>


  </div>

    <div class="artist-wiki prose mt-4">
      <p>Filipino artist known for using provocative dark humour and <a class="dtext-link dtext-wiki-link tag-type-0" href="/wiki_pages/when_you_see_it">hiding little details</a> in their images.</p><h4>Original characters</h4><ul><li><a class="dtext-link dtext-wiki-link tag-type-4" href="/wiki_pages/mookie_%28khyle.%29">Mookie</a> (used as their avatar)</li><li><a class="dtext-link dtext-wiki-link tag-type-4" href="/wiki_pages/tired_girl_%28khyle.%29">tired girl</a></li></ul>
    </div>

    <label class="flex items-center gap-1 font-header font-bold cursor-pointer select-none">
      <input type="checkbox" class="toggle-switch" x-model="blacklist.enabled" x-effect="$el.indeterminate = blacklist.partiallyEnabled" data-shortcut="b">
      Blacklisted
    </label>

    <span class="whitespace-nowrap post-count" x-text="blacklist.blacklistedPostCount"></span>
